- 1156 files · ~971,968 words

## Summary
- 8134 nodes · 13699 edges · 631 communities (574 shown, 57 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 86% EXTRACTED · 14% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1914 edges (avg confidence: 0.95)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `c4460af7`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
